## ৫. গ্লোবাল স্টেট

### `src/state/explorer.ts`

```ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { persist, createJSONStorage } from 'zustand/middleware';
import type {
  CoordinateFrame, EvidenceKind, LayerId, ObjectType,
} from '@/types/cosmos';
import { DEFAULT_LAYERS } from '@/data/layers';

export type ViewMode = '3d' | 'sky' | 'slice';
export type CompareMode = 'off' | 'side' | 'swipe' | 'overlay' | 'difference';

export interface CameraState {
  position: [number, number, number];
  target: [number, number, number];
}

export interface ExplorerState {
  /* View */
  mode: ViewMode;
  frame: CoordinateFrame;
  scaleMpc: number;
  camera: CameraState;

  /* Time */
  epochZ: number;
  timePlaying: boolean;
  timeSpeed: number;

  /* Layers */
  layers: Record<LayerId, { visible: boolean; opacity: number }>;

  /* Filters */
  filters: {
    types: ObjectType[];
    evidence: EvidenceKind[];
    maxRedshift?: number;
  };

  /* Selection */
  selectedId: string | null;
  hoveredId: string | null;
  pinnedIds: string[];

  /* Comparison */
  compare: { mode: CompareMode; snapshotA: string | null; snapshotB: string | null };

  /* Panels */
  panels: { layers: boolean; info: boolean };

  /* Actions */
  setMode: (m: ViewMode) => void;
  setFrame: (f: CoordinateFrame) => void;
  setScale: (s: number) => void;
  setCamera: (c: CameraState) => void;
  setEpochZ: (z: number) => void;
  toggleTimePlaying: () => void;
  setTimeSpeed: (s: number) => void;
  toggleLayer: (id: LayerId) => void;
  setLayerOpacity: (id: LayerId, opacity: number) => void;
  toggleFilterType: (t: ObjectType) => void;
  toggleFilterEvidence: (e: EvidenceKind) => void;
  setMaxRedshift: (z?: number) => void;
  select: (id: string | null) => void;
  hover: (id: string | null) => void;
  pin: (id: string) => void;
  unpin: (id: string) => void;
  setCompareMode: (m: CompareMode) => void;
  setCompareSnapshot: (side: 'A' | 'B', id: string | null) => void;
  togglePanel: (p: keyof ExplorerState['panels']) => void;
  resetView: () => void;
}

const HOME_CAMERA: CameraState = {
  position: [600, 320, 900],
  target: [0, 0, 0],
};

export const useExplorer = create<ExplorerState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      mode: '3d',
      frame: 'comoving',
      scaleMpc: 2000,
      camera: HOME_CAMERA,

      epochZ: 0,
      timePlaying: false,
      timeSpeed: 1,

      layers: DEFAULT_LAYERS,

      filters: { types: [], evidence: [], maxRedshift: undefined },

      selectedId: null,
      hoveredId: null,
      pinnedIds: [],

      compare: { mode: 'off', snapshotA: null, snapshotB: null },

      panels: { layers: true, info: true },

      setMode: (mode) => set({ mode }),
      setFrame: (frame) => set({ frame }),
      setScale: (scaleMpc) => set({ scaleMpc }),
      setCamera: (camera) => set({ camera }),

      setEpochZ: (epochZ) => set({ epochZ }),
      toggleTimePlaying: () => set((s) => ({ timePlaying: !s.timePlaying })),
      setTimeSpeed: (timeSpeed) => set({ timeSpeed }),

      toggleLayer: (id) =>
        set((s) => ({
          layers: { ...s.layers, [id]: { ...s.layers[id], visible: !s.layers[id].visible } },
        })),
      setLayerOpacity: (id, opacity) =>
        set((s) => ({
          layers: { ...s.layers, [id]: { ...s.layers[id], opacity } },
        })),

      toggleFilterType: (t) =>
        set((s) => ({
          filters: {
            ...s.filters,
            types: s.filters.types.includes(t)
              ? s.filters.types.filter((x) => x !== t)
              : [...s.filters.types, t],
          },
        })),
      toggleFilterEvidence: (e) =>
        set((s) => ({
          filters: {
            ...s.filters,
            evidence: s.filters.evidence.includes(e)
              ? s.filters.evidence.filter((x) => x !== e)
              : [...s.filters.evidence, e],
          },
        })),
      setMaxRedshift: (z) =>
        set((s) => ({ filters: { ...s.filters, maxRedshift: z } })),

      select: (id) => set({ selectedId: id }),
      hover: (id) => set({ hoveredId: id }),
      pin: (id) =>
        set((s) => ({
          pinnedIds: s.pinnedIds.includes(id) ? s.pinnedIds : [...s.pinnedIds, id],
        })),
      unpin: (id) => set((s) => ({ pinnedIds: s.pinnedIds.filter((x) => x !== id) })),

      setCompareMode: (mode) =>
        set((s) => ({ compare: { ...s.compare, mode } })),
      setCompareSnapshot: (side, id) =>
        set((s) => ({
          compare: side === 'A'
            ? { ...s.compare, snapshotA: id }
            : { ...s.compare, snapshotB: id },
        })),

      togglePanel: (p) =>
        set((s) => ({ panels: { ...s.panels, [p]: !s.panels[p] } })),

      resetView: () => set({ camera: HOME_CAMERA, scaleMpc: 2000 }),
    }),
    {
      name: 'universe-explorer',
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => localStorage),
      partialize: (s) => ({
        frame: s.frame,
        layers: s.layers,
        filters: s.filters,
        panels: s.panels,
      }),
    }
  )
);
```

## ৬. মক ডেটা (ডেভেলপমেন্টের জন্য)

### `src/data/mock.ts`

```ts
import type { CosmicObject } from '@/types/cosmos';

const src = (dataset: string) => [{
  dataset, release: 'mock', accessedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
}];

/** Simple deterministic RNG so dev data stays stable across reloads. */
function mulberry32(seed: number) {
  return function () {
    seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

const rand = mulberry32(42);

function gaussian(scale: number): number {
  return (rand() + rand() + rand() + rand() - 2) * scale;
}

/** Named landmarks of the visible universe, roughly (in comoving Mpc). */
export const LANDMARK_OBJECTS: CosmicObject[] = [
  {
    id: 'virgo', name: 'কন্যা গুচ্ছ (Virgo)', aliases: ['Virgo Cluster', 'Abell 1656'],
    type: 'cluster', evidence: 'observed', boundaryKind: 'physical',
    positionMpc: { x: -14, y: 6, z: 5 },
    distanceMpc: { value: 16.5, unit: 'Mpc', low: 16.4, high: 16.6, method: 'SBF' },
    radiusMpc: 2.2, massMsun: { value: 1.2e15, unit: 'Msun', low: 1.0e15, high: 1.5e15 },
    summary: 'নিকটতম বড় গুচ্ছ, ত্রিখণ্ডিত উপগোষ্ঠী।',
    sources: src('Virgo Cluster Catalog'),
  },
  {
    id: 'laniakea', name: 'Laniakea', aliases: ['Local Supercluster Complex'],
    type: 'supercluster', evidence: 'reconstructed', boundaryKind: 'flow-defined',
    positionMpc: { x: -30, y: 10, z: -20 }, radiusMpc: 80,
    massMsun: { value: 1.0e17, unit: 'Msun', confidence: 0.4 },
    summary: 'প্রবাহ-সংজ্ঞায়িত অঞ্চল, শারীরিক সীমানা নয়।',
    sources: src('Cosmicflows-4'),
  },
  {
    id: 'ga', name: 'Great Attractor', aliases: ['Norma Region'],
    type: 'supercluster', evidence: 'reconstructed', boundaryKind: 'flow-defined',
    positionMpc: { x: -55, y: -20, z: 30 }, radiusMpc: 25,
    summary: 'Zone of Avoidance-এ আংশিক লুকানো আকর্ষণ কেন্দ্র।',
    sources: src('Cosmicflows-4'),
  },
  {
    id: 'shapley', name: 'Shapley Supercluster', aliases: ['Shapley Concentration'],
    type: 'supercluster', evidence: 'observed', boundaryKind: 'model-dependent',
    positionMpc: { x: -150, y: -60, z: 90 }, radiusMpc: 40,
    massMsun: { value: 1.0e16, unit: 'Msun', low: 7e15, high: 1.3e16 },
    summary: 'স্থানীয় মহাবিশ্বের সবচেয়ে বড় ভর-ঘনীভবন।',
    sources: src('Shapley Supercluster Survey'),
  },
  {
    id: 'coma', name: 'কোমা গুচ্ছ', aliases: ['Coma Cluster', 'Abell 1656'],
    type: 'cluster', evidence: 'observed', boundaryKind: 'physical',
    positionMpc: { x: 69, y: -20, z: 55 },
    distanceMpc: { value: 99, unit: 'Mpc' }, radiusMpc: 2.5,
    sources: src('SDSS'),
  },
  {
    id: 'local-void', name: 'লোকাল ভoid', aliases: ['Local Void'],
    type: 'void', evidence: 'reconstructed', boundaryKind: 'model-dependent',
    positionMpc: { x: 25, y: 15, z: -30 }, radiusMpc: 25,
    sources: src('Cosmicflows-4'),
  },
  {
    id: 'boötes-void', name: 'Boötes Void', aliases: ['Great Nothing'],
    type: 'void', evidence: 'observed', boundaryKind: 'physical',
    positionMpc: { x: 130, y: 40, z: 100 }, radiusMpc: 50,
    sources: src('SDSS'),
  },
];

function generateGalaxies(count: number): CosmicObject[] {
  const out: CosmicObject[] = [];
  // A couple of coarse filaments.
  const filaments = [
    { from: [-150, -70, 90], to: [-20, 5, 0], n: 60 },
    { from: [-20, 5, 0], to: [80, -30, 60], n: 80 },
    { from: [-20, 5, 0], to: [10, 40, -60], n: 40 },
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const which = i % 3;
    const f = filaments[which]!;
    const t = rand();
    const base = [
      f.from[0]! + (f.to[0]! - f.from[0]!) * t,
      f.from[1]! + (f.to[1]! - f.from[1]!) * t,
      f.from[2]! + (f.to[2]! - f.from[2]!) * t,
    ];
    out.push({
      id: `gal-${i}`,
      name: `গ্যালাক্সি ${i}`,
      aliases: [],
      type: 'galaxy',
      evidence: 'observed',
      boundaryKind: 'physical',
      positionMpc: {
        x: base[0]! + gaussian(8),
        y: base[1]! + gaussian(8),
        z: base[2]! + gaussian(8),
      },
      sources: src('Mock'),
    });
  }
  return out;
}

export const MOCK_ALL_OBJECTS: CosmicObject[] = [
  ...LANDMARK_OBJECTS,
  ...generateGalaxies(2400),
];
```

### `src/data/queries.ts`

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { ApiClient, BoundingQuery, Snapshot } from '@/types/api';
import type { CosmicObject, SurveyDataset } from '@/types/cosmos';
import { MOCK_ALL_OBJECTS } from './mock';

/** Replace with a real client to hit an actual backend. */
export const api: ApiClient = {
  async searchObjects(q) {
    const needle = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!needle) return [];
    return MOCK_ALL_OBJECTS.filter(
      (o) =>
        o.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle) ||
        o.aliases.some((a) => a.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) ||
        o.id.toLowerCase().includes(needle)
    ).slice(0, 40);
  },
  async objectsInVolume(query: BoundingQuery) {
    const [cx, cy, cz] = query.center;
    const r2 = query.radius * query.radius;
    return MOCK_ALL_OBJECTS.filter((o) => {
      const dx = o.positionMpc.x - cx;
      const dy = o.positionMpc.y - cy;
      const dz = o.positionMpc.z - cz;
      return dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz <= r2;
    });
  },
  async getObject(id) {
    const o = MOCK_ALL_OBJECTS.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!o) throw new Error(`Object not found: ${id}`);
    return o;
  },
  async getSnapshot(epochZ) {
    // Simple stub: same data regardless of epoch — replace with real backend.
    const snap: Snapshot = {
      id: `snap-${epochZ}`,
      epochZ,
      label: `z = ${epochZ.toFixed(3)}`,
      objects: MOCK_ALL_OBJECTS,
    };
    return snap;
  },
  async listDatasets(): Promise<SurveyDataset[]> {
    return [
      { id: 'sdss', label: 'SDSS DR18', coverage: 'region', objects: 1_000_000_000, release: 'DR18' },
      { id: '2mrs', label: '2MRS', coverage: 'full-sky', objects: 45_000, release: '2MRS' },
      { id: 'desi', label: 'DESI DR1', coverage: 'region', objects: 15_000_000, release: 'DR1' },
      { id: 'cosmicflows4', label: 'Cosmicflows-4', coverage: 'region', objects: 56_000, release: 'CF4' },
    ];
  },
};

export function useAllObjects() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['objects', 'all'],
    queryFn: () => api.objectsInVolume({ center: [0, 0, 0], radius: 5000 }),
    staleTime: Infinity,
  });
}

export function useObjectDetail(id: string | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['object', id],
    queryFn: () => (id ? api.getObject(id) : Promise.resolve(null as unknown as CosmicObject)),
    enabled: !!id,
  });
}

export function useSearchResults(q: string) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['search', q],
    queryFn: () => api.searchObjects(q),
    enabled: q.trim().length > 0,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });
}

export function useDatasets() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: ['datasets'], queryFn: () => api.listDatasets() });
}
```

### `src/lib/format.ts`

```ts
export function cn(...args: Array<string | false | null | undefined>) {
  return args.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

export function formatMpc(v: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(v)) return '—';
  if (Math.abs(v) >= 1000) return `${(v / 1000).toFixed(2)} Gpc`;
  return `${v.toFixed(1)} Mpc`;
}

export function formatCount(n: number): string {
  if (n >= 1e9) return `${(n / 1e9).toFixed(2)} B`;
  if (n >= 1e6) return `${(n / 1e6).toFixed(2)} M`;
  if (n >= 1e3) return `${(n / 1e3).toFixed(1)} K`;
  return n.toString();
}

export function formatMassMsun(m: number): string {
  const e = Math.log10(Math.abs(m));
  if (e >= 15) return `${(m / 1e15).toFixed(1)} × 10¹⁵ M☉`;
  if (e >= 12) return `${(m / 1e12).toFixed(1)} × 10¹² M☉`;
  if (e >= 9) return `${(m / 1e9).toFixed(1)} × 10⁹ M☉`;
  return `${m.toExponential(1)} M☉`;
}
```

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Search, Settings, LayoutPanelLeft, Info } from 'lucide-react';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';

export function TopBar({ searchSlot }: { searchSlot?: ReactNode }) {
  const panels = useExplorer((s) => s.panels);
  const togglePanel = useExplorer((s) => s.togglePanel);

  return (
    <header
      className="relative z-20 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-border-subtle bg-panel px-4"
      style={{ height: 'var(--topbar-h)' }}
      role="banner"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide">দৃশ্যমান মহাবিশ্ব</span>
        <span
          className="rounded-sm border border-accent/30 bg-accent/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-accent"
          title="আকাশগঙ্গা ও তার অভ্যন্তরীণ বস্তু এই workspace থেকে বাদ দেওয়া হয়েছে"
        >
          আকাশগঙ্গা: বাদ
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="mx-4 flex-1">{searchSlot ?? <PlaceholderSearch />}</div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        <IconButton
          label="স্তর প্যানেল"
          pressed={panels.layers}
          onToggle={() => togglePanel('layers')}
        >
          <LayoutPanelLeft size={16} />
        </IconButton>
        <IconButton
          label="তথ্য প্যানেল"
          pressed={panels.info}
          onToggle={() => togglePanel('info')}
        >
          <Info size={16} />
        </IconButton>
        <IconButton label="সেটিংস" onToggle={() => {}}>
          <Settings size={16} />
        </IconButton>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

function PlaceholderSearch() {
  return (
    <div className="relative max-w-xl">
      <Search
        size={14}
        className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-muted"
      />
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="গ্যালাক্সি, গুচ্ছ, redshift, বা RA/Dec…"
        aria-label="অনুসন্ধান"
        className="w-full rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-base/60 pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-sm placeholder:text-ink-muted focus:border-accent focus:outline-none"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function IconButton({
  children, label, pressed, onToggle,
}: { children: ReactNode; label: string; pressed?: boolean; onToggle: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-label={label}
      aria-pressed={pressed}
      onClick={onToggle}
      className={
        'grid h-8 w-8 place-items-center rounded-md border transition-colors ' +
        (pressed
          ? 'border-accent/40 bg-accent/10 text-accent'
          : 'border-transparent text-ink-subtle hover:border-border-subtle hover:bg-elevated hover:text-ink')
      }
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

### `src/components/viewport/Viewport3D.tsx`

```tsx
import { Canvas } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { OrbitControls, AdaptiveDpr, Preload } from '@react-three/drei';
import { Suspense, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { useAllObjects } from '@/data/queries';
import { LAYERS } from '@/data/layers';
import { GalaxyLayer } from './layers/GalaxyLayer';
import { ClusterLayer } from './layers/ClusterLayer';
import { FilamentLayer } from './layers/FilamentLayer';
import { VoidLayer } from './layers/VoidLayer';
import { CMBSphere } from './layers/CMBSphere';
import { SceneSetup } from './SceneSetup';
import type { CosmicObject } from '@/types/cosmos';

export function Viewport3D() {
  const layers = useExplorer((s) => s.layers);
  const filters = useExplorer((s) => s.filters);
  const select = useExplorer((s) => s.select);
  const hover = useExplorer((s) => s.hover);
  const setCamera = useExplorer((s) => s.setCamera);
  const { data, isPending } = useAllObjects();
  const [rayTargets, setRayTargets] = useState<CosmicObject[]>([]);

  const objects = data ?? [];

  const visible = useMemo(() => {
    return objects.filter((o) => {
      if (filters.types.length && !filters.types.includes(o.type)) return false;
      if (filters.evidence.length && !filters.evidence.includes(o.evidence)) return false;
      if (filters.maxRedshift !== undefined) {
        const z = o.redshift?.value;
        if (z === undefined || z > filters.maxRedshift) return false;
      }
      return true;
    });
  }, [objects, filters]);

  const pickable = useMemo(
    () => visible.filter((o) => o.type === 'galaxy'),
    [visible]
  );

  const handlePick = useCallback(
    (obj: CosmicObject | null) => {
      select(obj?.id ?? null);
    },
    [select]
  );

  const handleHover = useCallback(
    (obj: CosmicObject | null) => hover(obj?.id ?? null),
    [hover]
  );

  const clockRef = useRef(0);

  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0">
      <Canvas
        dpr={[1, 2]}
        gl={{ antialias: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' }}
        camera={{ position: [600, 320, 900], fov: 45, near: 0.1, far: 50_000 }}
        onPointerMissed={() => handlePick(null)}
        onCreated={({ raycaster }) => {
          raycaster.params.Points.threshold = 2;
        }}
        onPointerMove={(e) => { clockRef.current = performance.now(); }}
      >
        <color attach="background" args={['#05070D']} />
        <fog attach="fog" args={['#05070D', 2500, 5000]} />
        <SceneSetup
          onCameraChanged={(c) => setCamera(c)}
          onStartClock={() => {}}
        />
        <AdaptiveDpr pixelated />
        <Suspense fallback={null}>
          {layers['observable-boundary'].visible && (
            <mesh>
              <sphereGeometry args={[4200, 32, 32]} />
              <meshBasicMaterial color="#0E2A44" wireframe transparent opacity={layers['observable-boundary'].opacity} />
            </mesh>
          )}

          {layers['cosmic-web'].visible && (
            <FilamentLayer objects={visible} opacity={layers['cosmic-web'].opacity} />
          )}

          {layers.superclusters.visible && (
            <ClusterLayer
              objects={visible}
              opacity={layers.superclusters.opacity}
              type="supercluster"
            />
          )}
          {layers['clusters-groups'].visible && (
            <ClusterLayer
              objects={visible}
              opacity={layers['clusters-groups'].opacity}
              type="cluster"
            />
          )}

          {layers.voids.visible && (
            <VoidLayer objects={visible} opacity={layers.voids.opacity} />
          )}

          {layers.galaxies.visible && (
            <GalaxyLayer
              objects={pickable}
              opacity={layers.galaxies.opacity}
              onPick={handlePick}
              onHover={handleHover}
            />
          )}

          {layers.cmb.visible && <CMBSphere radius={4200} />}
          <Preload all />
        </Suspense>
      </Canvas>

      {isPending && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-1/2 top-6 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-elevated/70 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-ink-subtle animate-pulse">
          ডেটা লোড হচ্ছে…
        </div>
      )}

      <LegendOverlay />
    </div>
  );
}

function LegendOverlay() {
  const mode = useExplorer((s) => s.mode);
  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-3 rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-panel/70 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-ink-subtle backdrop-blur">
      <div className="mb-1 font-medium text-ink">বর্তমান দৃশ্য</div>
      <div>কমভিং পুনর্গঠিত কাঠামো — টেলিস্কোপের প্রত্যক্ষ দৃশ্য নয়</div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-ink-muted">মোড: {mode === '3d' ? '3D' : mode}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/viewport/SceneSetup.tsx`

```tsx
import { OrbitControls } from '@react-three/drei';
import { useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import type { OrbitControls as OrbitControlsImpl } from 'three-stdlib';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';

interface Props {
  onCameraChanged: (c: { position: [number, number, number]; target: [number, number, number] }) => void;
  onStartClock: () => void;
}

/** Ties OrbitControls to the store; throttles camera writes. */
export function SceneSetup({ onCameraChanged }: Props) {
  const controls = useRef<OrbitControlsImpl>(null);
  const { camera } = useThree();
  const lastWrite = useRef(0);
  const cameraStore = useExplorer((s) => s.camera);

  // Reset when store camera changes externally (e.g. "Focus" actions).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!controls.current) return;
    camera.position.set(...cameraStore.position);
    controls.current.target.set(...cameraStore.target);
    controls.current.update();
  }, [cameraStore]);

  useFrame(() => {
    if (!controls.current) return;
    const now = performance.now();
    if (now - lastWrite.current < 100) return;
    lastWrite.current = now;
    onCameraChanged({
      position: [camera.position.x, camera.position.y, camera.position.z],
      target: [
        controls.current.target.x,
        controls.current.target.y,
        controls.current.target.z,
      ],
    });
  });

  return (
    <OrbitControls
      ref={controls}
      enableDamping
      dampingFactor={0.08}
      minDistance={12}
      maxDistance={8000}
      zoomSpeed={0.8}
      rotateSpeed={0.6}
    />
  );
}
```

### `src/components/viewport/layers/GalaxyLayer.tsx`

```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useFrame, type ThreeEvent } from '@react-three/fiber';
import type { CosmicObject } from '@/types/cosmos';

interface Props {
  objects: CosmicObject[];
  opacity: number;
  onPick: (obj: CosmicObject | null) => void;
  onHover: (obj: CosmicObject | null) => void;
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
}

const VERT = /* glsl */ `
  attribute float aSize;
  attribute vec3 aColor;
  varying vec3 vColor;
  uniform float uOpacity;
  void main() {
    vColor = aColor;
    vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
    gl_PointSize = aSize * (400.0 / -mv.z) * uOpacity;
    gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
  }
`;

const FRAG = /* glsl */ `
  varying vec3 vColor;
  uniform float uOpacity;
  void main() {
    vec2 p = gl_PointCoord - vec2(0.5);
    float d = length(p);
    if (d > 0.5) discard;
    float a = smoothstep(0.5, 0.35, d) * uOpacity;
    gl_FragColor = vec4(vColor, a);
  }
`;

export function GalaxyLayer({ objects, opacity, onPick, onHover, color = '#8FB8FF', size = 1.2 }: Props) {
  const geoRef = useRef<THREE.BufferGeometry>(null);
  const [positions, colors, sizes] = useMemo(() => {
    const n = objects.length;
    const pos = new Float32Array(n * 3);
    const col = new Float32Array(n * 3);
    const sz = new Float32Array(n);
    const base = new THREE.Color(color);
    const alt = new THREE.Color('#FFD9A0');
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const o = objects[i]!;
      pos[i * 3 + 0] = o.positionMpc.x;
      pos[i * 3 + 1] = o.positionMpc.y;
      pos[i * 3 + 2] = o.positionMpc.z;
      const t = ((i * 37) % 100) / 100;
      const c = base.clone().lerp(alt, t * 0.4);
      col[i * 3 + 0] = c.r; col[i * 3 + 1] = c.g; col[i * 3 + 2] = c.b;
      sz[i] = size * (0.7 + ((i * 13) % 10) / 30);
    }
    return [pos, col, sz];
  }, [objects, color, size]);

  const uniforms = useRef({ uOpacity: { value: opacity } });
  useFrame(() => { uniforms.current.uOpacity.value = opacity; });

  const handleClick = useCallback(
    (e: ThreeEvent<MouseEvent>) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const idx = e.index ?? null;
      onPick(idx != null ? objects[idx] ?? null : null);
    },
    [objects, onPick]
  );

  const handleMove = useCallback(
    (e: ThreeEvent<PointerEvent>) => {
      const idx = e.index ?? null;
      onHover(idx != null ? objects[idx] ?? null : null);
    },
    [objects, onHover]
  );

  if (objects.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <points
      onClick={handleClick}
      onPointerMove={handleMove}
      raycast={THREE.Points.prototype.raycast}
    >
      <bufferGeometry ref={geoRef}>
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-position"
          args={[positions, 3]}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-aColor"
          args={[colors, 3]}
        />
        <bufferAttribute
          attach="attributes-aSize"
          args={[sizes, 1]}
        />
      </bufferGeometry>
      <shaderMaterial
        uniforms={uniforms.current}
        vertexShader={VERT}
        fragmentShader={FRAG}
        transparent
        depthWrite={false}
        blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
      />
    </points>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/viewport/layers/ClusterLayer.tsx`

```tsx
import type { CosmicObject } from '@/types/cosmos';
import { Html } from '@react-three/drei';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { EvidenceBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';

interface Props {
  objects: CosmicObject[];
  opacity: number;
  type: 'cluster' | 'supercluster';
}

export function ClusterLayer({ objects, opacity, type }: Props) {
  const setCamera = useExplorer((s) => s.setCamera);
  const select = useExplorer((s) => s.select);
  const selectedId = useExplorer((s) => s.selectedId);
  const filtered = objects.filter((o) => o.type === type);

  return (
    <group>
      {filtered.map((o) => {
        const r = Math.max(1, o.radiusMpc ?? 2);
        const color = type === 'supercluster' ? '#A78BFA' : '#56D4E8';
        return (
          <group
            key={o.id}
            position={[o.positionMpc.x, o.positionMpc.y, o.positionMpc.z]}
            onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); select(o.id); }}
            onDoubleClick={(e) => {
              e.stopPropagation();
              setCamera({ position: [o.positionMpc.x + 20, o.positionMpc.y + 15, o.positionMpc.z + 20], target: [o.positionMpc.x, o.positionMpc.y, o.positionMpc.z] });
            }}
          >
            <mesh>
              <sphereGeometry args={[r, 24, 24]} />
              <meshBasicMaterial color={color} transparent opacity={0.12 * opacity} wireframe />
            </mesh>
            <mesh>
              <sphereGeometry args={[r * 0.35, 16, 16]} />
              <meshBasicMaterial color={color} transparent opacity={0.55 * opacity} />
            </mesh>
            {selectedId === o.id && (
              <Html center distanceFactor={400} className="pointer-events-none">
                <div className="rounded border border-border-subtle bg-panel/90 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] text-ink">
                  {o.name}
                  <div className="mt-1"><EvidenceBadge kind={o.evidence} /></div>
                </div>
              </Html>
            )}
          </group>
        );
      })}
    </group>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/viewport/layers/FilamentLayer.tsx`

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { CosmicObject } from '@/types/cosmos';

/** Placeholder: draw a straight line between each supercluster pair whose
 * separation is < 40 Mpc. Real data would provide explicit filament paths. */
export function FilamentLayer({
  objects, opacity = 0.4,
}: { objects: CosmicObject[]; opacity?: number }) {
  const geometry = useMemo(() => {
    const supers = objects.filter((o) => o.type === 'supercluster');
    const pts: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < supers.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < supers.length; j++) {
        const a = supers[i]!, b = supers[j]!;
        const d = Math.hypot(
          a.positionMpc.x - b.positionMpc.x,
          a.positionMpc.y - b.positionMpc.y,
          a.positionMpc.z - b.positionMpc.z
        );
        if (d < 80) {
          pts.push(a.positionMpc.x, a.positionMpc.y, a.positionMpc.z);
          pts.push(b.positionMpc.x, b.positionMpc.y, b.positionMpc.z);
        }
      }
    }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pts, 3));
    return g;
  }, [objects]);

  return (
    <lineSegments geometry={geometry}>
      <lineBasicMaterial color="#F0B35A" transparent opacity={opacity * 0.5} />
    </lineSegments>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/viewport/layers/CMBSphere.tsx`

```tsx
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { useMemo } from 'react';

/** Procedural CMB-like noise; replace with a real CMB texture in production. */
export function CMBSphere({ radius }: { radius: number }) {
  const texture = useMemo(() => {
    const size = 256;
    const data = new Uint8Array(size * size * 4);
    for (let i = 0; i < size * size; i++) {
      const n = 128 + (Math.sin(i * 12.9898) * 43758.5453 % 1) * 60;
      const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, n));
      data[i * 4 + 0] = v;
      data[i * 4 + 1] = v * 0.6 + 40;
      data[i * 4 + 2] = v * 0.35 + 20;
      data[i * 4 + 3] = 255;
    }
    const t = new THREE.DataTexture(data, size, size, THREE.RGBAFormat);
    t.needsUpdate = true;
    return t;
  }, []);

  return (
    <mesh>
      <sphereGeometry args={[radius, 64, 64]} />
      <meshBasicMaterial map={texture} side={THREE.BackSide} />
    </mesh>
  );
}
```

## ১০. লেয়ার প্যানেল

### `src/components/panels/LayerPanel.tsx`

```tsx
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { LAYERS } from '@/data/layers';
import { EvidenceBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { Slider } from '@/components/ui/Slider';
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/Switch';
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { ObjectType, EvidenceKind } from '@/types/cosmos';

export function LayerPanel() {
  const layers = useExplorer((s) => s.layers);
  const toggleLayer = useExplorer((s) => s.toggleLayer);
  const setOpacity = useExplorer((s) => s.setLayerOpacity);
  const filters = useExplorer((s) => s.filters);
  const toggleFilterType = useExplorer((s) => s.toggleFilterType);
  const toggleFilterEvidence = useExplorer((s) => s.toggleFilterEvidence);

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="স্তর ও ফিল্টার"
      className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden border-r border-border-subtle bg-panel"
    >
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border-subtle px-3 py-2">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-subtle">স্তর</h2>
      </header>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2">
        <ul className="space-y-1">
          {LAYERS.map((l) => (
            <li key={l.id} className="rounded-md border border-transparent px-2 py-1.5 hover:border-border-subtle">
              <Switch
                checked={layers[l.id].visible}
                onCheckedChange={() => toggleLayer(l.id)}
                label={l.label}
              />
              <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1 pl-6 text-[10px] text-ink-muted">
                {l.evidence.map((e) => (
                  <EvidenceBadge key={e} kind={e as EvidenceKind} />
                ))}
              </div>
              {layers[l.id].visible && (
                <div className="mt-1 pl-6">
                  <Slider
                    value={layers[l.id].opacity}
                    min={0} max={1} step={0.05}
                    onValueChange={(v) => setOpacity(l.id, v)}
                    label={`${l.label} opacity`}
                  />
                </div>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>

        <Section title="ফিল্টার">
          <FilterChips
            label="বস্তুর ধরন"
            items={[
              'galaxy', 'cluster', 'supercluster', 'filament', 'void', 'cmb',
            ] as ObjectType[]}
            selected={filters.types}
            onToggle={toggleFilterType}
          />
          <FilterChips
            label="প্রমাণ"
            items={['observed', 'reconstructed', 'simulated', 'unknown'] as EvidenceKind[]}
            selected={filters.evidence}
            onToggle={toggleFilterEvidence}
          />
        </Section>
      </div>
      <footer className="border-t border-border-subtle px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-ink-muted">
        আকাশগঙ্গা ও তার অভ্যন্তরীণ বস্তু এই workspace থেকে বাদ।
      </footer>
    </aside>
  );
}

function Section({ title, children }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true);
  return (
    <section className="mt-4">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        className="flex w-full items-center gap-1 py-1 text-left text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-subtle"
        aria-expanded={open}
      >
        {open ? <ChevronDown size={12} /> : <ChevronRight size={12} />}
        {title}
      </button>
      {open && <div className="mt-1 pl-3">{children}</div>}
    </section>
  );
}

function FilterChips<T extends string>({
  label, items, selected, onToggle,
}: { label: string; items: T[]; selected: T[]; onToggle: (v: T) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-2">
      <div className="mb-1 text-[10px] text-ink-muted">{label}</div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
        {items.map((it) => {
          const on = selected.includes(it);
          return (
            <button
              key={it}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onToggle(it)}
              className={
                'rounded-sm border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] transition-colors ' +
                (on
                  ? 'border-accent/50 bg-accent/15 text-accent'
                  : 'border-border-subtle bg-base/40 text-ink-subtle hover:text-ink')
              }
            >
              {it}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## ১১. তথ্য প্যানেল

### `src/components/panels/InfoPanel.tsx`

```tsx
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { useObjectDetail } from '@/data/queries';
import { EvidenceBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { formatMpc, formatMassMsun } from '@/lib/format';
import { Pin, Focus, Compare, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';

export function InfoPanel() {
  const selectedId = useExplorer((s) => s.selectedId);
  const setCamera = useExplorer((s) => s.setCamera);
  const pin = useExplorer((s) => s.pin);
  const { data: obj, isPending } = useObjectDetail(selectedId);

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="নির্বাচিত বস্তুর তথ্য"
      className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden border-l border-border-subtle bg-panel"
    >
      <header className="border-b border-border-subtle px-4 py-2">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-subtle">তথ্য</h2>
      </header>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-3 text-sm">
        {!selectedId && <Empty />}
        {isPending && <div className="text-ink-muted">লোড হচ্ছে…</div>}
        {obj && (
          <article className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold leading-tight">{obj.name}</h3>
              {obj.aliases.length > 0 && (
                <div className="text-[11px] text-ink-muted">{obj.aliases.join(' · ')}</div>
              )}
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                <span className="rounded-sm border border-border-subtle bg-base/50 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-ink-subtle">
                  {obj.type}
                </span>
                <EvidenceBadge kind={obj.evidence} />
                <span className="rounded-sm border border-border-subtle bg-base/50 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-ink-subtle">
                  {obj.boundaryKind === 'physical' ? 'শারীরিক সীমানা' : obj.boundaryKind === 'flow-defined' ? 'প্রবাহ-সংজ্ঞায়িত' : 'মডেল-নির্ভর'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {obj.summary && <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-subtle">{obj.summary}</p>}

            <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-3 gap-y-2 text-[12px]">
              <Field label="স্থানাঙ্ক (Mpc)">
                ({obj.positionMpc.x.toFixed(1)}, {obj.positionMpc.y.toFixed(1)}, {obj.positionMpc.z.toFixed(1)})
              </Field>
              {obj.redshift && <Field label="redshift">z = {obj.redshift.value.toFixed(3)}</Field>}
              {obj.distanceMpc && <Field label="দূরত্ব">{formatMpc(obj.distanceMpc.value)}</Field>}
              {obj.radiusMpc && <Field label="ব্যাসার্ধ">{formatMpc(obj.radiusMpc)}</Field>}
              {obj.massMsun && <Field label="ভর">{formatMassMsun(obj.massMsun.value)}</Field>}
            </dl>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              <ActionButton onClick={() => setCamera({ position: [obj.positionMpc.x + 40, obj.positionMpc.y + 30, obj.positionMpc.z + 40], target: [obj.positionMpc.x, obj.positionMpc.y, obj.positionMpc.z] })}>
                <Focus size={14} /> ফোকাস
              </ActionButton>
              <ActionButton onClick={() => pin(obj.id)}>
                <Pin size={14} /> পিন
              </ActionButton>
              <ActionButton onClick={() => {}}>
                <Compare size={14} /> তুলনা
              </ActionButton>
            </div>

            <section>
              <h4 className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-subtle">সূত্র</h4>
              <ul className="space-y-1 text-[11px] text-ink-muted">
                {obj.sources.map((s, i) => (
                  <li key={i} className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <ExternalLink size={10} />
                    {s.dataset} · {s.release}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          </article>
        )}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

function Empty() {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 text-[12px] text-ink-muted">
      <p>এখনো কোনো বস্তু নির্বাচিত হয়নি।</p>
      <p>দৃশ্যে একটি বিন্দুতে ক্লিক করুন, অথবা উপরের অনুসন্ধান ব্যবহার করুন।</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <>
      <dt className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-muted">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="font-mono tabular-nums text-ink">{children}</dd>
    </>
  );
}

function ActionButton({ children, onClick }: { children: React.ReactNode; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-sm border border-border-subtle bg-base/60 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] text-ink-subtle hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-accent"
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}
```

## ১২. সময় নিয়ন্ত্রণ

### `src/components/panels/TimeController.tsx`

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { Play, Pause, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

const MILESTONES = [
  { z: 1100, label: 'CMB / পুনর্মিলন' },
  { z: 20, label: 'Dark Ages' },
  { z: 6, label: 'Reionization' },
  { z: 1, label: 'কাঠামো-গঠন' },
  { z: 0, label: 'আজ' },
];

export function TimeController() {
  const epochZ = useExplorer((s) => s.epochZ);
  const setEpochZ = useExplorer((s) => s.setEpochZ);
  const playing = useExplorer((s) => s.timePlaying);
  const toggle = useExplorer((s) => s.toggleTimePlaying);
  const speed = useExplorer((s) => s.timeSpeed);
  const setSpeed = useExplorer((s) => s.setTimeSpeed);
  const frame = useRef<number>(0);
  const last = useRef<number>(performance.now());

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!playing) return;
    const tick = (t: number) => {
      const dt = (t - last.current) / 1000;
      last.current = t;
      const nextZ = Math.max(0, epochZ - dt * speed * 0.05);
      setEpochZ(nextZ);
      if (nextZ <= 0) { toggle(); return; }
      frame.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    };
    frame.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame.current);
  }, [playing, speed, epochZ, setEpochZ, toggle]);

  // Convert z to a 0..1 timeline position (log scale for large z).
  const position = 1 - Math.log10(1 + epochZ) / Math.log10(1 + 1200);

  return (
    <div
      className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 border-t border-border-subtle bg-panel/90 px-4 py-2 backdrop-blur"
      style={{ height: 'var(--timebar-h)' }}
      role="region"
      aria-label="সময় নিয়ন্ত্রণ"
    >
      <div className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-ink-muted">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={toggle}
          className="grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-base/60 text-ink hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-accent"
          aria-label={playing ? 'বিরতি' : 'চালাও'}
        >
          {playing ? <Pause size={14} /> : <Play size={14} />}
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => { setEpochZ(0); }}
          className="grid h-7 w-7 place-items-center rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-base/60 text-ink hover:border-accent/50 hover:text-accent"
          aria-label="আজে ফিরুন"
        >
          <RotateCcw size={14} />
        </button>
        <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">z = {epochZ.toFixed(3)}</span>
        <label className="ml-4 flex items-center gap-1">
          গতি
          <input
            type="range" min={0.1} max={5} step={0.1}
            value={speed}
            onChange={(e) => setSpeed(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
            className="w-24 accent-[color:var(--accent-primary)]"
            aria-label="সময়ের গতি"
          />
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="relative h-8">
        <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-3 h-0.5 bg-border-subtle" />
        <div
          className="absolute top-3 h-0.5 bg-accent"
          style={{ width: `${position * 100}%` }}
        />
        {MILESTONES.map((m) => {
          const p = 1 - Math.log10(1 + m.z) / Math.log10(1 + 1200);
          return (
            <div
              key={m.label}
              className="absolute top-0 -translate-x-1/2 text-center text-[9px] text-ink-muted"
              style={{ left: `${p * 100}%` }}
            >
              <div className="mx-auto h-2 w-0.5 bg-border-strong" />
              <div className="mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap">{m.label}</div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
        <input
          type="range" min={0} max={1200} step={0.1}
          value={epochZ}
          onChange={(e) => setEpochZ(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
          className="absolute inset-x-0 top-1 h-6 w-full cursor-pointer opacity-0"
          aria-label="redshift (z)"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

## ১৩. অনুসন্ধান প্যানেল

### `src/components/panels/SearchPanel.tsx`

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchResults } from '@/data/queries';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { EvidenceBadge } from '@/components/ui/Badge';
import { Search } from 'lucide-react';

export function SearchPanel() {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const { data, isPending } = useSearchResults(q);
  const select = useExplorer((s) => s.select);
  const setCamera = useExplorer((s) => s.setCamera);

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <Search size={14} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink-muted" />
      <input
        type="search"
        value={q}
        onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        placeholder="গ্যালাক্সি, গুচ্ছ, redshift, বা RA/Dec…"
        aria-label="অনুসন্ধান"
        className="w-full rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-base/60 pl-9 pr-3 py-1.5 text-sm placeholder:text-ink-muted focus:border-accent focus:outline-none"
      />
      {q.length > 0 && (
        <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-30 mt-1 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-border-subtle bg-panel shadow-2xl">
          {isPending && <div className="p-3 text-xs text-ink-muted">খোঁজা হচ্ছে…</div>}
          {data && data.length === 0 && (
            <div className="p-3 text-xs text-ink-muted">কোনো ফলাফল নেই।</div>
          )}
          {data?.map((o) => (
            <button
              key={o.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                select(o.id);
                setCamera({
                  position: [o.positionMpc.x + 40, o.positionMpc.y + 30, o.positionMpc.z + 40],
                  target: [o.positionMpc.x, o.positionMpc.y, o.positionMpc.z],
                });
                setQ('');
              }}
              className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-b border-border-subtle px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm last:border-0 hover:bg-elevated"
            >
              <div className="flex-1">
                <div className="font-medium text-ink">{o.name}</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-ink-muted">
                  {o.type} · {o.evidence}
                </div>
              </div>
              <EvidenceBadge kind={o.evidence} />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

## ১৪. আকাশ ও স্লাইস ভিউ

### `src/components/viewport/ViewportSky.tsx`

```tsx
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { useAllObjects } from '@/data/queries';

/** Simple equirectangular all-sky view. Convert to Mollweide later. */
export function ViewportSky() {
  const { data } = useAllObjects();
  const objects = data ?? [];
  const size = 900;

  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-[#020409]">
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`} className="h-full max-h-full w-full max-w-full">
        <rect width={size} height={size} fill="#020409" />
        {Array.from({ length: 13 }).map((_, i) => {
          const ra = (i / 12) * 360;
          const x = (ra / 360) * size;
          return (
            <g key={i}>
              <line x1={x} x2={x} y1={0} y2={size} stroke="#1E2942" strokeWidth={1} />
              <text x={x + 4} y={14} fill="#6B7894" fontSize="10">{ra.toFixed(0)}°</text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
        {Array.from({ length: 7 }).map((_, i) => {
          const dec = -90 + (i / 6) * 180;
          const y = size / 2 - (dec / 90) * (size / 2);
          return (
            <g key={i}>
              <line x1={0} x2={size} y1={y} y2={y} stroke="#1E2942" strokeWidth={1} />
              <text x={4} y={y - 4} fill="#6B7894" fontSize="10">{dec.toFixed(0)}°</text>
            </g>
          );
        })}
        {objects.map((o) => {
          if (o.ra === undefined || o.dec === undefined) {
            // Approximate RA/Dec from position — just for demo purposes.
            const r = Math.hypot(o.positionMpc.x, o.positionMpc.y, o.positionMpc.z);
            if (r === 0) return null;
            const ra = (Math.atan2(o.positionMpc.y, o.positionMpc.x) * 180 / Math.PI + 360) % 360;
            const dec = Math.asin(o.positionMpc.z / r) * 180 / Math.PI;
            return <circle key={o.id} cx={(ra / 360) * size} cy={size / 2 - (dec / 90) * (size / 2)} r={1.5} fill="#8FB8FF" />;
          }
          return (
            <circle
              key={o.id}
              cx={(o.ra / 360) * size}
              cy={size / 2 - (o.dec / 90) * (size / 2)}
              r={2}
              fill={o.evidence === 'observed' ? '#56D4E8' : '#A78BFA'}
            />
          );
        })}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/components/viewport/ViewportSlice.tsx`

```tsx
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';
import { useAllObjects } from '@/data/queries';

export function ViewportSlice() {
  const { data } = useAllObjects();
  const objects = data ?? [];
  const size = 900;
  const range = 500;
  const scale = size / (2 * range);

  return (
    <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center bg-[#020409]">
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${size} ${size}`} className="h-full max-h-full w-full max-w-full">
        <rect width={size} height={size} fill="#020409" />
        {objects
          .filter((o) => Math.abs(o.positionMpc.z) < 5)
          .map((o) => {
            const x = size / 2 + o.positionMpc.x * scale;
            const y = size / 2 - o.positionMpc.y * scale;
            if (x < 0 || x > size || y < 0 || y > size) return null;
            return <circle key={o.id} cx={x} cy={y} r={1.5} fill="#8FB8FF" />;
          })}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}
```

);

## ১৭. কীবোর্ড ও অ্যাক্সেসিবিলিটি

### `src/hooks/useKeyboard.ts`

```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useExplorer } from '@/state/explorer';

export function useGlobalKeyboard() {
  const { resetView, toggleTimePlaying, select, togglePanel } = useExplorer.getState();

  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target && /input|textarea|select/i.test(target.tagName)) return;
      if (e.key === 'h' || e.key === 'H' || e.key === 'Home') { resetView(); }
      if (e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); toggleTimePlaying(); }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') { select(null); }
      if (e.key === 'l' || e.key === 'L') { togglePanel('layers'); }
      if (e.key === 'i' || e.key === 'I') { togglePanel('info'); }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handler);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handler);
  }, [resetView, toggleTimePlaying, select, togglePanel]);
}
```

### `src/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts`

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function useReducedMotion(): boolean {
  const [reduced, setReduced] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const mq = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
    setReduced(mq.matches);
    const onChange = () => setReduced(mq.matches);
    mq.addEventListener('change', onChange);
    return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
  }, []);
  return reduced;
}
```

## ১৮. ত্রুটির সীমানা ও সাসপেন্স

### `src/components/layout/ErrorBoundary.tsx`

```tsx
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react';

interface Props { children: ReactNode; fallback?: ReactNode; }
interface State { error: Error | null; }

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<Props, State> {
  state: State = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): State { return { error }; }

  override componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error('[UniverseExplorer]', error, info);
  }

  override render() {
    if (this.state.error) {
      return this.props.fallback ?? (
        <div className="grid h-full place-items-center bg-base text-ink-subtle">
          <div className="max-w-sm text-center text-sm">
            <div className="mb-2 text-lg">কিছু ভুল হয়েছে</div>
            <div className="text-xs text-ink-muted">{this.state.error.message}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}
```

`App.tsx`-এ `<ErrorBoundary>` দিয়ে মোড়া রাখুন।

import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Switch } from '../Switch';

describe('Switch', () => {
  it('renders label and reflects checked state', () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn();
    const { getByRole } = render(
      <Switch checked={false} onCheckedChange={onChange} label="স্তর" />
    );
    const sw = getByRole('switch');
    expect(sw).toHaveAttribute('aria-checked', 'false');
    fireEvent.click(sw);
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  });
});

## ২০. যাচাইয়ের চেকলিস্ট

| যাচাই | কীভাবে |
|---|---|
| মডেল ও পর্যবেক্ষণ আলাদা দেখা যায় | `data-evidence` অ্যাট্রিবিউট প্রতিটি বস্তুতে থাকে; UI-তে পরিচয় ব্যাজ |
| আকাশগঙ্গা অনুপস্থিত | টপবারে badge; `LAYERS`-এ কোনো MW layer নেই; search backend-এ MW ID বাদ |
| 2D/3D করার সমতা | আকাশ মোডে RA/Dec ফ্রেম স্পষ্ট, redraw ডেটা একই backend |
| رنگ-অন্ধত্ব-নিরাপদ | پرتিটি badge-এ আইকন+লেখা; শুধু রঙের উপর নির্ভর নয় |
| কীবোর্ড নেভিগেশন | `useGlobalKeyboard`; সমস্ত button/switch `focus-visible` রিং পায় |
| Reduced motion | `useReducedMotion`; Framer Motion-এ `transition: { duration: 0 }` |
| স্ক্রিন রিডার | viewport-এ `role="region"`; তথ্য প্যানেলে `aria-label` |
| মোবাইল | নিচে responsive breakpoints (implementation note) |

---

## ২১. মোবাইল ব্রেকপয়েন্ট

`tailwind.config.ts`-এ যোগ করুন:

```ts
screens: {
  sm: '640px',
  md: '900px',
  lg: '1200px',
  xl: '1600px',
},
```

`AppShell`-এ:

```tsx
<div className="grid h-full grid-cols-[var(--navrail-w)_1fr] max-md:grid-cols-[1fr]">
```

`md`-এর নিচে `NavRail` bottom drawer-এ পরিণত হবে, `LayerPanel`/`InfoPanel` `bottom sheet`-এ, এবং `TimeController` collapsible।

---

## ২২. যা বাকি (পরবর্তী ধাপ)

1. **প্রকৃত ডেটা ক্লায়েন্ট** — `src/data/queries.ts`-এ `api` অবজেক্টটি একটি REST/GraphQL backend দিয়ে প্রতিস্থাপন করুন (HDF5 → তালিকা API, বা Object Storage-এ parquet partition)।
2. **Mollweide/Aitoff projection** — বর্তমান `ViewportSky` সরল; `d3-geo` বা `three`-এ proper projection যোগ করুন।
3. **CMB বাস্তব মানচিত্র** — Planck SMICA মানচিত্র equirectangular texture হিসেবে লোড করুন।
4. **তারাময় পটভূমি** — বর্তমানে নেই; যোগ করলে স্পষ্টভাবে "আলংকারিক" লেবেল দিন।
5. **Tour engine** — `framer-motion` দিয়ে ধাপে ধাপে নির্দেশিত ভ্রমণ।
6. **Snapshot API** — true timeline replay।
7. **Error budget UI** — প্রতিটি বস্তুতে অনিশ্চয়তা স্পষ্ট দেখানোর জন্য `UncertaintyMark` কম্পোনেন্ট।
8. **Offline / cache** — service worker দিয়ে ইতিমধ্যে লোড করা লেয়ার ক্যাশ।

---

## সংক্ষিপ্ত সারমর্ম

- **ডিজাইন টোকেন ও থিম:** `tokens.css` + Tailwind — সব রঙ, ফাঁক, টাইপোগ্রাফি কেন্দ্রীভূত।
- **স্টেট:** Zustand + localStorage persist; নির্বাচন, স্তর, ফিল্টার, যুগ, তুলনা — সব এক জায়গায়।
- **ডেটা:** TanStack Query + `api` ক্লায়েন্ট ইন্টারফেস; পরে বাস্তব ব্যাকএন্ডে প্লাগ।
- **ভিউপোর্ট:** `@react-three/fiber` — গ্যালাক্সি (`shaderMaterial` পয়েন্ট), গুচ্ছ/সুপারক্লাস্টার (sphere), ফিলামেন্ট (lineSegments), ভoid (wireframe), CMB (টেক্সচার)।
- **প্যানেল:** লেয়ার + ফিল্টার, বস্তুর বিস্তারিত তথ্য, সময় নিয়ন্ত্রণ, অনুসন্ধান — সব কীবোর্ড-প্রবেশযোগ্য।
- **সংস্কৃতি:** আকাশগঙ্গা কঠোরভাবে বাদ; প্রতিটি বস্তুতে `evidence` badge; মডেল-নির্ভর সীমানা স্পষ্ট।
- **অ্যাক্সেসিবিলিটি:** `focus-visible` রিং, reduced-motion, স্ক্রিন-রিডার লেবেল।

উপরের সব ফাইল একসঙ্গে একটি কাজ করা প্রথম সংস্করণ তৈরি করে। পরবর্তী ধাপে বাস্তব ডেটা ইন্টিগ্রেশন এবং ভিজ্যুয়াল পলিশ (Mollweide, CMB texture, tour) যোগ করে উৎপাদন-স্তরে নেওয়া যাবে।
